### [143] [Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) for aerodynamic prediction: a comparison with MLPs and GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27126)
*Miguel Jaraiz,Fermin Gutierrez,Pablo Yeste,Miguel Sánchez-Domínguez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了KAN（Kolmogorov-Arnold网络）在流体动力学代理建模中的性能，特别是预测亚音速和跨音速翼型表面压力分布的任务。结果表明，KAN性能与MLP相当但略逊一筹，最佳性能由GNN实现但训练时间更长。KAN虽模型复杂度低、训练快，但存在训练不稳定问题，且性能高度依赖超参数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学界对KAN是否能够在符号回归、通用机器学习、自然语言处理和计算机视觉等领域超越传统MLP存在争议，本文旨在通过流体动力学代理建模这一具体领域，客观评估KAN的性能及其与MLP、GNN的对比。

Method: 以预测亚音速和跨音速翼型表面压力分布（压力系数）为基准任务，系统比较KAN、MLP和GNN三种架构在训练效率、模型复杂度、预测精度和稳定性等方面的表现。

Result: KAN在预测压力系数方面表现良好，能跨马赫数和攻角进行插值，但其性能略逊于经过适当训练的MLP；GNN取得最佳性能但训练时间更长。KAN模型复杂度最低、训练速度最快，但存在训练不稳定性问题，且性能高度依赖超参数优化。

Conclusion: 在流体动力学代理建模中，KAN并未展现出优于MLP的显著优势，其性能与MLP相当但略逊一筹，不支持KAN supremacy的说法；GNN虽性能最优但训练成本高；KAN的实际应用受限于训练不稳定性和对超参数的敏感性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) have recently been introduced as a (deep) neural network architecture whose trainable parameters adapt the activation functions, instead of the coefficients of the affine transformations at the core of traditional architectures such as deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). This architecture builds on the Kolmogorov-Arnold theorem, which endows it with universal approximation properties. While the advent of KANs has been received with excitement, there is a current debate about the possible KAN supremacy over deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) for classic fields such as symbolic regression, generic-purpose machine learning, natural language processing or computer vision. Here we assess the performance of KANs --and its nuanced comparison against MLPs and graph neural networks (GNNs)-- in the realm of fluid dynamics surrogate modelling. To that aim, we consider the task of predicting the surface pressure distribution over subsonic and transonic airfoils, a canonical task in aerodynamics. Our results show that KAN models show good performance in predicting the whole pressure coefficients and is able to interpolate across Mach numbers and angles of attack, however its performance is comparable --marginally inferior-- to a suitably trained MLP, where best performance is achieved by a GNN at the expense or requiring lengthier training. While the optimal KAN model have typically much lower complexity than MLP and GNN --hence resulting in faster training--, we find that KANs suffer from training instabilities, and their performance is highly dependent on a proper hyperparameter optimisation.

</details>


### [144] [fTNN: a tensor neural network for fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27140)
*Qingkui Ma,Hehu Xie,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出fTNN，一种用于分数阶拉普拉斯问题的确定性张量神经网络子空间方法，通过几何自适应积分分割、边界奇异性增强基函数和时空可分离网络架构，在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟任务上显著优于现有fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数阶拉普拉斯问题的数值方法（如fPINN和蒙特卡洛）在处理强边界奇异性和长时间模拟时精度不足，亟需一种高精度、完全确定性的计算框架。

Method: 1) 采用几何自适应积分分割，将分数阶拉普拉斯分解为奇异近场、规则中场和解析远场三部分；2) 近场使用Gauss-Jacobi求积，中场使用Gauss求积，远场使用确定性角度求积；3) 构造边界奇异性感知的试验函数，自动选择主导指数；4) 对时间依赖问题设计时空可分离神经网络，结合交替神经网络子空间优化策略。

Result: 数值实验表明，fTNN在多个基准测试上达到高精度，特别是在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟任务上显著优于现有fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。

Conclusion: fTNN通过完全确定性的积分框架和奇异性感知的基函数设计，为分数阶拉普拉斯问题提供了高精度且计算高效的求解方案。

Abstract: We develop the fTNN, a deterministic tensor neural network subspace method for problems involving the fractional Laplacian on bounded domains, taking the fractional Poisson equation and time-dependent fractional advection-diffusion equation as typical representatives. The work employs a geometry-adapted integration split featuring a spatially dependent near-field radius, which decomposes the fractional Laplacian into three contributions: a singular near field, a regular interior far field, and an analytical exterior far field. Then the singular radial integrals are treated by Gauss-Jacobi quadrature, the regular radial integrals by Gauss quadrature, and the angular variables by deterministic angular quadrature, yielding a fully deterministic integration framework of the fractional Laplacian operator. To accurately resolve low-regularity solutions and the associated loss functional, we construct boundary-singularity-aware trial functions enriched with explicit boundary features, and propose two strategies for automatically selecting the leading exponent and evaluating the loss function from the singularity structure induced by the fractional operator, or jointly by the fractional operator and the source term. For time-dependent fractional PDEs, we design a spatiotemporally separable neural network that factorizes the time-space residual into a sum of low-dimensional temporal and spatial integrals, and we integrate this representation with an alternating neural network subspace optimization strategy for efficient training. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework attains high accuracy on the tested benchmarks and improves substantially over existing fPINN and Monte Carlo baselines, particularly for problems with strong boundary singularities and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [145] [Stochastic Gradient Optimization with Model-Assisted Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27171)
*Jonne Pohjankukka,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型辅助抽样的梯度估计框架，将小批量梯度下降视为有限总体抽样问题，通过引入辅助梯度预测模型来构建更高效的梯度估计器，在71-86%的实验中获得性能提升，并在AdamW等动量优化器中仅需约一半训练轮次即可达到更好的泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的小批量梯度下降引入梯度噪声，现有方差缩减方法（如SVRG、SAG）和自适应优化器虽然能缓解这一问题，但可能引入额外计算开销。本文旨在弥合机器学习优化与抽样调查理论之间的鸿沟，利用抽样调查的视角来构建更高效的梯度估计器。

Method: 提出模型辅助抽样框架，将数据集视为固定有限总体，梯度视为基于样本的估计量；通过引入辅助梯度预测模型来构建更高效的梯度估计器，均匀抽样作为无辅助信息时的特例。该框架可轻松集成到现有优化器中而不改变其动力学特性。

Result: 在合成数据集和六个基准数据集上的实验表明，71-86%的实验获得性能提升，尤其在中维输入空间上效果显著；采用AdamW等动量优化器时，所提估计器在大约一半的训练轮次内即可达到明显更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型辅助抽样框架能有效降低随机梯度估计中的方差，提升优化效率，且易于集成到现有优化器中使用，是一种实用且高效的梯度估计改进方案。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

</details>


### [146] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecallRisk-BERT，一个多任务学习框架，利用54,165条FDA医疗设备召回记录（2002-2025年），结合PubMedBERT文本表征和结构化分类特征嵌入，同时预测召回严重等级（I/II/III）和根本原因类别（9类）。LightGBM文本-表格模型在单任务严重性预测中表现最优（准确率0.963，宏F1 0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974），多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线，风险排序与根本原因严重性模式高度一致（rho=0.983）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDA医疗设备召回记录数量快速增长，导致召回后分类、严重性评估和根本原因解读面临挑战。现有研究大多分别处理召回发生预测或根本原因分析，而联合建模召回严重性和根本原因类别的工作有限。

Method: 使用54,165条openFDA医疗设备召回记录（2002-2025年）。首先评估经典机器学习和基于boosting的模型。然后开发RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，结合PubMedBERT文本表征和产品代码、法规编号、医学专业等结构化分类特征的嵌入表征，同时预测召回严重等级（三级）和根本原因类别（9类）。

Result: 单任务严重性预测中，LightGBM文本-表格配置表现最优（准确率0.963，宏F1 0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974）。多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线。模型风险排序与根本原因严重性模式高度一致（斯皮尔曼rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格学习可以支持可扩展的召回后分类、监管决策支持和基于模型的根本原因风险分析。

Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [147] [Automating Potential-based Reward Shaping with Vision Language Model Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27180)
*Henrik Müller,Daniel Kudenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-PBRS框架，利用轻量级视觉语言模型（VLM）偏好学习来学习势函数，实现基于势能的奖励塑形，在保持最优策略集不变的同时提升样本效率并防止奖励破解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励问题中缺乏中间反馈，导致探索困难和信用分配问题；而朴素奖励塑形可能引发奖励破解。现有的基于势能的奖励塑形（PBRS）虽保证最优策略集不变，但需要专家设计启发式势函数，成本高且不通用。

Method: 使用轻量级VLM对图像对进行偏好排序，基于这些偏好标签训练势函数模型，再将该势函数用于PBRS框架中对原始奖励进行塑形。由于采用小规模VLM，虽偏好标签精度略低，但计算效率高且仍能加速学习。

Result: 在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中验证，VLM-PBRS显著提升了样本效率，并能有效防止奖励破解。实验还揭示了VLM偏好标签准确率与样本效率改进之间的关联。

Conclusion: 首次将VLM偏好学习应用于PBRS势函数合成，提供了一种低成本、原则性的奖励塑形方案，既保留了最优策略集，又提升了样本效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are inherently challenging for reinforcement learning agents as they lack intermediate feedback to guide exploration and to correctly attribute the sparse success rewards to relevant parts of the trajectory. Naive reward shaping can induce reward hacking, yielding policies that exploit auxiliary signals instead of solving the intended task. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) guarantees preservation of the optimal policy set, but requires the definition of a heuristic potential function over the state space. In this work, we introduce the VLM-guided PBRS framework VLM-PBRS that learns the potential function directly from vision language model (VLM) feedback. We query a lightweight VLM to obtain preferences over image pairs and train a model of the potential function using these preferences. As this approach is based on potential-based reward shaping, it preserves the original optimal policies, and removes the need for expert-designed reward shaping terms. Because large VLMs are prohibitively expensive to invoke repeatedly during policy learning, we employ smaller, more computationally efficient VLMs. Although the resulting preference labels are less accurate, empirical evidence shows that the preference labels can still be used to accelerate learning. We validate our method empirically in the Meta-World and Franka Kitchen environments and highlight the connection between VLM preference label accuracy and sample efficiency improvements. Our contributions are threefold: (1) the first application of VLM preference-based learning to synthesize a potential function for PBRS, (2) a principled, low-cost solution that leverages small VLMs, and (3) extensive empirical demonstration of improved sample efficiency and robustness to reward hacking.

</details>


### [148] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化相关性度量（NRM）框架的新颖归因方法，通过分析事件时序图神经网络（ETGNNs）中所有事件相关变量的完整信息流，解决了现有解释方法仅关注部分信息路径（忽略事件诱导变量）的问题，并在流行病追踪、社会动力学和政治事件网络数据集上取得了优于现有方法的可解释性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETGNNs解释方法仅追踪从事件相关嵌入到输出的部分信息流，忽略了通过事件诱导变量（event-induced variables）的重要路径，而这些变量在捕捉长时序依赖中发挥核心作用。因此需要一种能够分析完整信息流的新颖归因方法。

Method: 基于归一化相关性度量（NRM）框架，扩展了模块化分解过程，实现对ETGNNs复杂架构中所有事件相关变量（包括事件嵌入和事件诱导变量）的完整信息流量化，支持跨层潜变量的可比性分析和事件间高阶交互分析。

Result: 在两个合成数据集（流行病追踪、社会动力学）和一个真实世界政治事件网络数据集上的定性和定量实验表明，该方法在解释性能上持续优于现有方法，并生成更符合人类可解释性的解释。

Conclusion: 提出的基于NRM的完整信息流归因方法有效克服了ETGNNs现有解释方法的局限性，通过覆盖事件诱导变量的信息路径，实现了更全面、更可解释的归因分析。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [149] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了图神经网络的设计空间、谱域与空域统一原理、Weisfeiler-Leman层次下的表达能力，并在12个应用领域中逐一评估图结构收益与成本，揭示异质性、规模、时序动态等跨领域共性挑战，以及基准偏差、部署与榜单脱节等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络已成为处理关系数据的默认模型，但关键问题不再是消息传递是否有用，而是图结构在哪些场景下值得其计算开销、在哪些场景下不必要。本文旨在通过统一设计空间和跨领域实证对比，为这一问题提供系统性答案。

Method: 围绕单一设计空间组织领域，从共享第一性原理推导谱域与空域公式，将表达能力关联至Weisfeiler-Leman层次并明确当前架构的可/不可区分范围。在此基础上，对12个应用领域逐一指定图构建方式及成本、识别主导架构族及原因，区分真实收益与弱基线、有利数据划分导致的人为假象，并进行跨领域对比分析。

Result: 跨领域对比揭示若干重复性模式：异质性和规模几乎在所有领域都削弱了相同模型；时序图始终比静态图更难；在公开排行榜上表现最好的架构很少是实际部署的架构。过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性成为决定模型能否被采纳的约束条件，而非可简单勾选的清单。

Conclusion: 图结构收益具有高度领域依赖性，基准评估中存在显著偏差（弱基线、有利划分），排行榜性能不等于部署可行性。未来研究需关注异质性、时序动态与规模等跨领域共性挑战，并将鲁棒性、公平性等约束条件纳入模型设计核心考量。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

</details>


### [150] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FisherSketch，一种无需微调即可评估共享词汇表LLM家族中源模型与目标任务迁移性的方法，通过计算头Fisher对齐的余弦相似度，仅需单次流式扫描即可生成紧凑任务签名（16KB），在激活相似度失效时仍能有效指导源选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学序列领域（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组），候选语料库共享词表但预测目标不同，存在"激活-黑暗"现象：表示相似性指标（如CKA）在缺乏标签误差几何假设时失效，而经典更新几何指标（如Fisher矩阵）在词汇表规模下计算成本过高。需要一种既计算高效又能捕捉迁移相关更新结构的方法。

Method: 证明在共享输出头设置下，表示度量（如CKA）对迁移不可识别——模型可能共享相同表示但头更新正交。关键恒等式：头Fisher对齐恰好是联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦，分解为激活、误差和耦合因子。FisherSketch通过单次流式扫描直接估计该余弦，生成K=128,256头Fisher对齐所需的16KB任务签名（m=4096）和192KB每任务流状态。

Result: FisherSketch在共享参数和内部层验证中表现有效；在Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer偏移实验中，当激活相似度无法区分任务时，FisherSketch仍能保持信息量。

Conclusion: FisherSketch提供了一种轻量级、可存储于模型哈希旁的源选择诊断工具，其签名和边际量还能用于研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是它们的耦合驱动。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [151] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜在协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。模型扩展至类别相关、相关且非零均值的潜在结构，证明了在高维极限下微观训练过程收敛到由有效协方差控制的确定性常微分方程。稳定性分析揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关性可将弱方向提升至可学习阈值之上，而过强的相关性会破坏恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解GAN分析假设无条件信号和 diagonal 潜在协方差，忽略了实际数据中常见的类别相关、相关和非零均值的潜在结构。本文旨在推广这一框架，研究结构化潜在协方差下GAN训练的动力行为。

Method: 采用高维极限下的随机矩阵理论，将微观训练过程收敛到确定性ODE；通过匹配协方差特化进行稳定性分析，得到模式级可解区间；使用数值模拟和MNIST、FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10实验验证。

Result: 所有异质性通过概率加权有效二阶矩进入动力学；学习起始于有效特征值超过下阈值，完全恢复要求所有相关有效模式保持在区间内；低秩相关性增强弱方向可学习性，强相关性则破坏稳定。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了结构化潜在协方差在GAN学习过程中的信号增强作用，为理解GAN训练动力学和设计更好的生成器协方差提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [152] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RSPC语料库（包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit长距离关系帖子），并在多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务上基准测试了7个微调Transformer和5个大语言模型，发现Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类上最优（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上最优（Macro-F1=0.519），并揭示了焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间的强关联，推动从个体中心向上下文感知的心理健康建模转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康研究通常将精神疾病建模为孤立现象，忽略了人际关系背景。本文旨在通过引入关系上下文来捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关的人际触发因素。

Method: 构建了Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC)，包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit长距离关系帖子，标注类别包括情绪障碍（焦虑和抑郁）、关系压力触发因素以及关系阶段指示。采用7个微调Transformer模型和5个大语言模型进行多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务。

Result: Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务上表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务上表现最强（Macro-F1=0.519）。发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系上下文的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从个体中心向上下文感知的心理健康建模范式转变，能够捕捉困扰的社会和时间动态。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [153] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个来自Instagram和Reddit的博彩广告新数据集，包含人工标注的操纵性和欺骗性广告实践及其解释性注释，分析了博彩广告中常用的说服策略及其对用户心理健康的潜在影响，并提出了可用于浏览器插件和自动化网络爬虫的检测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上博彩应用推广显著增加，许多广告使用可能误导用户、鼓励冒险行为并影响心理健康的说服技巧，然而由于缺乏公开标注数据集，自动化检测操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的研究十分有限。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个主流社交媒体平台收集博彩相关广告数据，进行人工标注操纵性和欺骗性广告实践，同时提供人工标注理由以支持可解释性方法研究，并分析常用说服策略及其对心理健康的影响。

Result: 构建了一个包含分类标签和人类推理解释的博彩广告标注数据集，识别了常见说服策略，并提出了一个可用于浏览器插件和自动爬虫的检测框架。

Conclusion: 该数据集和框架能够支持可解释的操纵性广告检测研究，并具备实用价值，如开发警告用户的可视化插件和帮助监管机构监测网络博彩推广的工具。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [154] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了时间序列预测中"更大模型带来更好精度"的主流观点，提出通过优化数据预处理（如上下文长度、归一化、正则化等）而非扩大模型规模，即可达到甚至超越复杂架构的性能。以Ridge回归为测试平台，在8个基准上发现三个关键模式：最优回溯长度具有序列特异性且与预测长度非线性相关；局部归一化优于全局归一化；同一数据集中不同序列的最佳超参数各异。优化后的模型超越传统线性模型，并在6/8基准上击败Transformer/MLP/CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究过度追求更大规模的架构（从专业Transformer到通用基础模型），认为模型容量是提升精度的关键。作者持相反立场，认为通过调优预处理而非扩大模型规模，可以更高效地缩小性能差距。

Method: 以Ridge回归（具有闭式解和可解释权重）为测试平台，系统搜索上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强四个预处理维度，在8个标准基准上实验，并分析最优超参数的模式特征。

Result: 发现了三个统计模式：1）最优回溯长度高度依赖具体序列，与预测长度的关系呈非单调幂律分布（幂指数从+0.46到-0.19不等）；2）基于学习到的上下文尾部比例做局部归一化几乎总是更优选择；3）同一数据集内不同序列的最优超参数存在分歧，跨序列共享程度从全局共享到独立学习各不相同。优化后的Ridge模型超越现有线性预测器，并在6/8基准上超过Transformer/MLP/CNN。

Conclusion: 预处理优化是比模型缩放更高效、更具解释性的提升路径；优化得到的超参数还可作为数据本身的诊断工具，揭示大模型隐含吸收的数据结构信息。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [155] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对从观测数据中学习支配方程的理论基础问题，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为微分方程的自然度量，建立了线性及Lipschitz/Hölder连续非线性ODE类的可辨识性边界，并推导了样本复杂度的度量熵估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学机器学习方法虽能从数据中学习支配方程，但关于真实ODE能否被唯一且稳定识别（可辨识性）的理论条件尚未建立，且缺乏对样本复杂度的定量分析。

Method: 1) 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量；2) 在此度量下建立线性ODE及Lipschitz/Hölder连续非线性ODE类的可辨识性边界；3) 利用度量熵估计推导样本复杂度下界。

Result: 给出了不同类型ODE结构方程的可辨识性条件，明确了何时两个不同方程可从解数据中区分；得到了相应ODE类的度量熵估计及样本复杂度上界。

Conclusion: 为从多解观测中唯一且稳定识别支配ODE提供了理论保障，填补了该领域可辨识性与样本复杂度分析的空缺。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [156] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RLAIF的端到端框架，用于生成可迁移的求职搜索查询。研究发现，在高度对抗性的奖励表面下，策略优化常利用LLM评判标准的缺陷产生退化解。实验表明，无评论家优化器的性能主要取决于奖励塑形而非算法选择；GRPO对虚假奖励信号尤为敏感，而引入基于规则的奖励下限可有效缓解这一问题。训练阶段的奖励模型将性能提升放大了2.4倍，证实成功关键在于奖励塑形而非优化器选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职搜索平台依赖低维查询接口，难以捕捉候选档案的高维复杂性。现有方法无法生成既能抽象掉求职者特定标识、又能保留可泛化资质的可迁移搜索查询词。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架；对比多种优化器（RLOO、REINFORCE++、GRPO）；引入确定性规则奖励下限；使用交叉族评估法官进行质量评估。

Result: 无评论家优化器性能主要取决于奖励塑形；GRPO对虚假奖励信号敏感；引入规则奖励下限带来+0.147质量提升；训练奖励模型将性能增益放大了2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功从根本上依赖于强制执行奖励塑形规范，而非选择不同的优化器。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [157] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真度迁移学习框架，结合轻量级物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习、前馈神经网络和少量实验数据，实现了板状结构中损伤的准确定位与尺寸估计，在R²评分上分别超过0.93和0.99，为导波结构健康监测提供了计算高效且实用的深度学习解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导波结构健康监测中深度学习模型的实际部署面临两大障碍：标注实验数据有限，以及生成大规模高保真仿真数据集的计算成本过高。

Method: 提出多保真度迁移学习框架，包括：(1) 使用一维时域谱元模型生成大量轻量级合成数据集进行预训练；(2) 基于卷积自编码器的深度特征学习；(3) 前馈神经网络进行损伤定位与尺寸估计；(4) 利用少量标注实验数据进行迁移学习以适配实验域。

Result: CAE迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于CNN对应方法；损伤定位R²>0.93，损伤尺寸估计R²>0.99；在未见过的数据上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架被证明是一种准确、计算高效且实用的解决方案，适用于实际导波结构健康监测应用。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [158] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度均衡（GEQ）与Blackwell可逼近性在算法意义上是等价的，并由此建立了GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等在线学习框架之间的联系，同时给出了GEQ的必要充分条件以及不同约束决策集下GEQ之间的归约关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GEQ与遗憾最小化等在线学习框架存在相似性，但先前研究表明GEQ误差与遗憾是不可比较的目标，因而缺乏对GEQ在整个在线学习领域中的准确定位。本文旨在厘清GEQ与这些框架之间的精确关系。

Method: 通过构造双向的黑盒归约（black-box reduction），证明GEQ问题与Blackwell可逼近问题可以在互不损失渐近误差率的情况下相互转换；进而利用已知的可逼近性与遗憾最小化、校准之间的等价关系，推导GEQ与这些框架的等价性；同时分析GEQ的必要充分条件，建立无约束与有约束决策集下不同GEQ概念之间的归约。

Result: 证明了GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性在算法意义下等价；基于此等价性，GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等框架等价；归约是高效的，可将乐观、强自适应性等精细保证从遗憾最小化迁移至GEQ；给出了GEQ的充要条件及不同约束决策集下GEQ概念的归约关系。

Conclusion: GEQ并非与现有在线学习框架无关，而是与Blackwell可逼近性、遗憾最小化、校准等核心框架算法等价，从而将GEQ准确定位于在线学习版图中，并为后续迁移更丰富的理论保证提供了工具。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与Top-k稀疏自编码器兼容的稀疏正则化方法（ℓ1惩罚和ℓ1/ℓ2比例惩罚），在不牺牲重构质量的前提下提升了视觉基础模型表示的单语义性，证明了硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化是互补的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Top-k SAE虽然避免了ℓ1惩罚的缺点，但存在固定预算k和过拟合等问题，且未与显式稀疏正则化结合。作者希望引入正则化来弥补这些局限性。

Method: 在Top-k选择前的激活上施加两种正则化：对未选中的单元施加ℓ1惩罚，以及对批活跃单元施加尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比例惩罚。两种惩罚仅作用于批次内至少被Top-k选中一次的单元。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型和多种k值下，两种正则化均在不降低重构质量的前提下持续提升单语义性。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚还能将信息集中到更少的潜在单元上，使重构对推理时k的选择更鲁棒，并改善小预算线性探针性能。

Conclusion: 硬性架构稀疏性（Top-k）与软性稀疏正则化并非互斥，而是互补的，二者结合能带来更好的表示解释性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [160] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，通过MMBench2数据集和覆盖感知技术，实现了幻觉检测与缓解的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式世界模型虽能生成逼真的动作可控未来帧，但频繁出现幻觉（视觉流畅但偏离真实动态），作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，轻量级数据驱动信号可同时检测并引导缓解。

Method: 构建427小时、210任务的MMBench2数据集，训练350M参数世界模型；识别三种幻觉模式（感知、动作边缘化、场景发散），开发对应预测信号；提出覆盖感知采样技术（训练时）和基于好奇心奖励的在线数据收集策略（部署时）。

Result: 幻觉预测器能准确预测模型失败位置；覆盖感知采样和好奇心奖励驱动的微调方法仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可将预训练世界模型适配到全新未见环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质是数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的同一信号也可用于缓解幻觉。

Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

</details>


### [161] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出误差条件神经求解器(ENS)，将PDE残差场作为网络输入而非优化目标，使网络能够读取自身误差空间结构并学习迭代校正策略，在四个PDE系列中取得最高预测精度（湍流Kolmogorov流提升10倍），避免混合方法的高计算成本，并在分布偏移下展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将求解视为纯统计任务，训练后难以修正自身约束违反和外推；混合方法虽通过梯度下降最小化PDE残差来提升物理正确性，但继承了经典优化器的计算成本和不稳定性，且在病态系统中残差最小化并非重建精度的可靠代理。

Method: 将PDE残差场作为网络直接输入（而非优化目标），使网络能读取自身误差的空间结构，学习迭代更新策略以逐步修正预测。通过理论和实验证明数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中可能不可靠，解释混合方法常出现低残差但预测不准的现象。

Result: 在四个PDE系列的大部分设置中达到最高预测精度，湍流Kolmogorov流相比基线提升10倍；避免混合方法的高计算成本；在分布偏移（零样本参数变化、跨方程迁移）下泛化良好，且在病态区域优势最大。

Conclusion: 将残差作为输入而非优化目标是更有效的范式，ENS在精度、计算效率和泛化能力上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [162] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器(ArBG)，一种替代基于流的玻尔兹曼生成器框架的新方法，通过自回归建模克服了归一化流在表达能力、拓扑约束和计算效率上的局限性，在分子平衡态采样任务上显著优于现有方法，并在大规模可迁移模型Robin上实现了零样本能量误差降低60%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的玻尔兹曼生成器主要依赖归一化流(NFs)，但离散时间流受严格可逆性约束导致表达能力有限，连续时间流则面临计算昂贵的似然估计问题。因此需要一种能突破这些局限的新框架。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器(ArBG)，采用自回归建模框架替代基于流的方法，规避了流的拓扑约束，支持推理时的序列化干预，并利用在大语言模型中有效的架构来提升可扩展性。同时基于该框架训练了1.32亿参数的可迁移模型Robin。

Result: 在所有基准测试上，ArBG显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在10残基Chignolin等较大肽体系上表现突出。Robin模型在8残基体系上将零样本能量误差E-W₂降低了超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归建模框架为分子平衡态采样提供了一条超越归一化流范式的新路径，兼具更好的表达能力、拓扑灵活性和计算效率，在大规模可迁移模型上展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [163] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励塑造方法，在不依赖标准答案的情况下，利用基于分数的优化任务训练LLM，在启发式竞赛和精确求解基准测试上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖标准答案分配奖励，限制了其在无标准答案任务（如启发式优化、分数型竞赛）中的应用。作者希望在不依赖标准答案的情况下，利用分数型任务训练LLM，并使其能力迁移到精确求解任务上。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，针对连续奖励场景中出现的两个关键问题（scale dominance和frequency dominance），设计了基于实例级比较的校准奖励塑造方法：通过实例级归一化解决分数尺度不统一问题，通过强调排名靠前的解并为其他有效解保留有界反馈来解决频率主导问题。

Result: 在AtCoder Heuristic Contest上训练，在ALE-Bench上Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414分别提升8.9%和9.4%；在LiveCodeBench和USACO上分别平均提升2.4%和3.5%。相比之下，使用原始分数的基线方法虽能提升ALE评分，但无法迁移到精确求解基准。

Conclusion: 基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以成为无需标准答案的有效训练环境，不仅能提升分数型任务表现，还能迁移到精确求解任务，证明了该类任务在通用编码能力训练中的价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代式数据生成流程，通过隔离随行为线性缩放的特征样本（而非简单二元对比样本），更好地解耦模型特征，实现对语言模型中谄媚行为的检测与引导。该方法在线性可分离子空间中发现谄媚特征，在检测、确定性评分和鲁棒引导方面达到或超越基线方法，且计算开销更低、可解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法依赖清晰展现期望/非期望行为的对比样本对，这些数据对的质量直接影响可解释性框架能否可靠检测行为相关的模型特征，进而影响引导能力。简单二元样本对不足以充分解耦复杂行为特征。

Method: 提出迭代式数据生成流程，通过隔离随行为线性缩放的特征样本（cascading samples）取代简单二元对比样本，在激活空间中构建线性可分离子空间，从而更好地解耦与行为相关的级联线性特征。

Result: 该方法在谄媚行为检测、确定性评分和鲁棒引导任务上达到或超越LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示等基线方法，同时计算需求更低，可解释性保证更强。

Conclusion: 通过级联样本发现的谄媚特征形成线性可分离子空间，能选择更清晰对应目标行为的模型激活，为模型行为的可解释性分析和控制提供更高效、更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [165] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出超越准确率的智能体性能评估框架，涵盖六大维度（构念效度、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架相对重要性、人机协作提升），并以CORE-Bench Hard（代码可重复性基准）为案例验证了即使准确率饱和后这些维度仍能提供有意义的洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估范式过于聚焦准确率，当基准准确率饱和后往往选择直接替换更难的版本，忽视了其他关键性能维度，缺乏更全面的评估方法。

Method: 以CORE-Bench Hard为案例，通过引入改进基准CORE-Bench v1.1和分布外测试套件CORE-Bench OOD来评估构念效度威胁；测量效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架性能；开展小规模随机实验量化人机协作提升。

Result: 发现了在较弱智能体下难以预见的构念效度威胁；准确率饱和后CORE-Bench v1.1仍可用于效率、可靠性、模型性能和脚手架性能测量；人机协作在真实计算可重复性任务上实现约两倍的统计显著加速（可能被低估，因五分之一的纯人工复现超时）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种比主流以准确率为中心的评估范式更为严格的替代方案，展示了多维度评估在准确率饱和后的持续价值。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [166] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调模型中的拒绝机制（refusal）与顺从人格（compliant persona）存在交互：顺从人格方向会调控拒绝方向的表达。通过干预Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中的这两个方向，发现顺从人格抑制拒绝，而拒绝方向的恢复作用仅限于后层，表明拒绝在计算完成后还需经过人格方向的"门控"才能表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究分别识别了激活空间中的拒绝方向和人格方向，但未探究两者的相互作用。本文旨在揭示这两个机制如何协同工作，特别是顺从人格如何调控拒绝行为。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中提取顺从模型人格方向和拒绝方向，通过干预（steering、projecting out）这两个方向，观察拒绝率的变化，并分析不同层级的干预效果。

Result: 顺从人格方向抑制拒绝：Llama中拒绝率从97%降至2%；在后层重新引入拒绝方向可部分恢复拒绝，但前层无效；在后层投影去除人格方向可恢复基准拒绝率，随机方向则不能。

Conclusion: 拒绝机制并非孤立的线性方向，其表达受到人格方向的"门控"调控。这种门控发生在后层的表达阶段，而非拒绝方向的计算阶段。因此，仅将拒绝视为单一孤立方向会忽略其对人格的依赖。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [167] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlgoEvolve，一个利用大语言模型驱动的进化框架，用于在算法交易领域自动生成、评估和迭代改进可执行的交易策略。系统展现出涌现性的自适应交易逻辑，并引入元进化外环自动优化提示搜索启发式，在非平稳、高噪声的交易环境中持续合成优于人工设计的交易程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的语义进化方法主要应用于静态编程基准测试，尚未在算法交易这一噪声大、非平稳、高度不连续的复杂领域得到验证。本文旨在探索LLM语义进化能否在如此动态和不确定的环境中有效生成和改进交易策略。

Method: 提出AlgoEvolve双环进化框架：内环使用LLM作为语义变异算子，生成、评估并迭代改进用Python表达的交易策略；外环（元进化）对指导内环程序合成的提示进行进化优化，自动发现平衡探索与利用、减少零交易失败的搜索启发式。

Result: 系统涌现出自适应市场状态的交易逻辑（如自主切换交易规则）；元进化外环发现的搜索启发式在平衡探索与利用、减少零交易失败方面一致优于初始人工设计的提示指令。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义进化方法为复杂环境下的持续程序合成提供了可行途径，验证了该范式在算法交易这一动态领域中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [168] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的比较分析流程，用于大规模研究AI代理互操作性标准的治理结构，通过对ERC-8004（去中心化）和Google A2A（公司主导）两个标准进行实证分析，发现治理形式影响主题关注点，但两种体制都表现出相似的参与不平等和社区碎片化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI代理协议激增，但塑造其互操作性标准的治理结构在实证上仍缺乏系统研究，需要对不同治理模式下的社会技术权力结构进行大规模分析。

Method: 构建了基于LLM的比较分析流程，集成了自动化标注、神经主题建模和多层网络分析，对4,323条治理参与记录进行分析。

Result: 治理形式影响实质性关注点；两种体制都存在相似的参与不平等和社区碎片化；去中心化治理中话语对齐更密集，表明开放治理可能促进更大的主题收敛。

Conclusion: LLM辅助方法可以推进技术治理的实证研究，为设计更公平的自主AI标准提供了启示。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [169] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于知识图谱、具有溯源意识的多智能体框架，整合了来自Reddit、WebMD和FDA不良事件报告系统的数据，用于九种抗抑郁药物。通过LLM实体识别管线（F1最高达0.973），发现患者社区数据与FDA报告存在显著差异，且许多不良事件在社区中出现的时间远早于官方记录。基于Neo4j知识图谱保留了每条声明的来源，确保监管事实与患者经验相互区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者越来越多地在线获取药物信息，但精神科药物的安全性知识分散在两处：权威但抽象的监管不良事件记录，以及经验贴近但未经验证的患者叙述。在精神科领域，信息缺乏上下文的整合会加剧恐惧、反安慰剂效应和停药行为。因此需要一种既能融合两种证据源、又不混淆事实与轶闻的系统化方法。

Method: 构建了基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论以及20年的FDA FAERS数据（覆盖9种抗抑郁药）。使用大语言模型实体识别管线（经医师标注基准评测），以ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA为术语体系构建Neo4j知识图谱，保留每条声明的溯源信息。

Result: LLM实体识别F1最高达0.969（药物）和0.973（疾病）。两个社区平台之间的Jaccard相似度高达0.905，远高于它们与FDA报告的一致性。以舍曲林为例，许多不良事件在社区来源中出现的日期比FDA记录早数百天。

Conclusion: 患者生成数据构成了部分独立的药物安全信号。溯源感知的整合为精神科药物信息提供了更可审计的路径，其有用性和患者获益有待前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [170] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合认知神经科学中漂移扩散模型（DDM）的增强型Elo评分系统（DD-Elo），通过整合棋局中每一步的决策级数据来捕捉技能的快速波动，从而克服传统Elo系统仅依赖比赛结果导致的响应滞后问题。理论推导证明DD-Elo与传统Elo的偏差有界，实验表明其能更快适应技能变化，具有可解释性、高响应性和向后兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo评分系统仅依赖比赛结果，存在固有的响应滞后问题，无法捕捉比赛过程中细致的棋艺质量信息。而将每一步棋的信息融入评分调整中面临着棋局状态空间庞大且噪声显著的挑战。

Method: 借鉴认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型（DDM），将棋艺表达建模为决策过程，利用每一步棋的级别数据来捕捉技能的快速波动，在此基础上构建DD-Elo评分系统。

Result: 理论推导证明DD-Elo与标准Elo的偏差有界；大量实验表明DD-Elo在适应技能变化的速度上优于传统Elo系统。

Conclusion: DD-Elo为国际象棋评分生态系统提供了一种可解释、高响应性且向后兼容的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [171] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算治理模型，要求AI代理在高风险行动（如临床处方、软件部署）前，需提供由独立权威来源认证的声明意图和前提条件，执行由确定性策略评估，并记录在防篡改日志中，而非监控其推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理即将执行不可逆的高风险行动，现有方法（监控推理过程）在实际治理中不可行，人类机构对强大自治行为体的治理模式是要求独立认证证据而非监控内心活动。

Method: 形式化了一种计算治理模型：代理保留规划与推理的完全自治权，但对高风险的执行无权威；执行条件由独立权威认证、加密绑定声明意图、由确定性策略评估；决策记录于防篡改日志。

Result: 提供了概念验证实现，并通过软件部署和临床处方案例进行了说明。

Conclusion: 该模型能够在不限制AI代理推理能力的前提下，通过对执行环节施加可验证的约束来实现安全治理，适用于高风险自主行动场景。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [172] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COrigami，一个端到端的AI驱动管道，通过自然语言生成折纸折痕图案，该管道结合了几何约束（平面可折叠性）和美学评估（强化学习自主评价循环），实现了数学严谨的协同创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在可验证解的问题上取得了成功，但在生成同时满足严格几何约束和主观视觉美感的物理艺术作品方面仍面临挑战。计算折纸作为一个数学上严谨的环境，将艺术设计置于平面可折叠性的方程中，为应对这一挑战提供了理想场景。

Method: COrigami管道包括五个步骤：从自然语言生成语义草图、计算基础排布、求解平面可折叠折痕图案、塑造平面折叠后的折痕图案、以及使用强化学习驱动的自主美学评估循环来优化生成的模型。

Result: 该系统作为高效的协作助手，生成结构化的初始点供人类艺术家进一步扩展和塑造，展示了AI系统能够满足多目标物理约束以实现可靠的、数学基础的协同创造力。

Conclusion: 通过将算法优化与自主美学批评相结合，本工作展示了AI系统如何在满足多目标物理约束的前提下，实现数学上严谨的协同创造性。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [173] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在代码智能体领域，验证（verification）比生成（generation）更困难，并沿着可扩展性、忠实性和鲁棒性三个维度刻画验证信号质量。通过测试验证器、评分验证器、用户即验证器和自动智能体验证器四种奖励设计方案，实验表明验证需与生成器共同进化，固定奖励函数无法持续有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直觉认为验证比生成容易，但当前代码智能体恰恰相反——生成复杂方案已不困难，但可靠地验证方案是否符合人类意图成为更棘手的问题。每一个可构建的验证器都只是人类意图的代理（proxy），而非意图本身，这导致验证面临双重困境：意图本质上未充分指定，难以忠实核查；模型训练中优化会放大代理与意图之间的差距，表现为奖励黑客或信号饱和。

Method: 从可扩展性（scalability）、忠实性（faithfulness）和鲁棒性（robustness）三个维度刻画验证信号质量。研究了四种奖励构建方法：针对通用代码任务的测试验证器、前端任务的评分验证器、真实世界智能体任务中用户即验证器、长期任务中的自动智能体验证器。在不同任务类型和策略能力水平下进行了深入分析和实验。

Result: 实验表明，针对性的验证设计可以有效抑制奖励黑客攻击，提升任务完成质量，并在多个内部和公开基准测试上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 没有固定的奖励函数能够随着策略能力持续增长而保持有效；验证必须与生成器共同进化（co-evolve）。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [174] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对能源领域缺乏智能体评测基准的空白，构建了包含243个专家级问题的真实能源市场分析评测环境，配备实时API、监管检索等专业工具集，对闭源和开源LLM智能体进行了多维度评估，并公开相关工件的支持复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准已覆盖金融、编程、法律、药物发现等领域，但能源领域评测仍停留在静态知识回忆层面。能源行业需要实时数据检索、专业监管和市场知识以及多步定量推理，现有基准无法满足这一关键需求。

Method: 构建了243个专家策划的三大类问题（市场数据检索分析、知识检索解释、高级定量建模与决策分析），为智能体配备可配置的领域工具集（实时电力市场API、监管文档检索、关税数据库、资产优化模型、RAG），采用多维度评估协议（方法正确性、答案准确性、属性对齐、来源有效性）并针对类别路由评分标准。

Result: 对闭源和开源LLM进行了对比分析，揭示了模型能力与领域工具在高风险专业领域中的交互方式。关键工件已公开发布以支持可复现性和未来研究。

Conclusion: 填补了能源领域智能体评测的空白，为高专业性、实时性、多步推理需求的行业评估提供了可复现的基准框架，且工具增强对于能源市场分析任务至关重要。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [175] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）评估滞后于模型发展，现有基准局限于孤立任务，未能有效衡量跨模态信息整合能力。作者通过审视现有评估方法和基准分类，识别出四个评估缺口：时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意，认为填补这些缺口对衡量多模态智能的真实进展至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型能力快速提升，但评估体系发展滞后，现有基准多聚焦孤立任务，无法揭示模型跨模态信息整合的真实水平，亟需系统性的评估框架来识别模型的能力边界。

Method: 通过审视现有MLLMs评估手段，回顾已有基准分类体系，系统性地识别评估缺口。

Result: 识别出四个关键评估缺口：时空连贯性（temporal-spatial coherence）、物理世界理解（physical world understanding）、多模态一致性（multimodal consistency）和选择性注意（selective attention）。

Conclusion: 填补上述评估缺口是衡量多模态智能真实进展、揭示模型能力边界的关键途径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [176] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenFinGym是一个统一量化金融Agent开发环境，覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等多个任务，提供自动化任务构建、容器化运行时、论文交易引擎等功能，支持SFT和RL后训练集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型Agent在量化金融中的评估分散在孤立任务上，且忽视了基准任务的金融相关性。金融工作流本质上是多阶段的，涵盖预测、策略构建、风险管理和交易等相互依赖的任务。现有平台通常聚焦单一任务，可能夸大Agent能力，无法揭示其在泛化、市场交互和金融决策方面的弱点。

Method: OpenFinGym构建了统一的gym环境，包含：(1) 自动化任务构建管道，将量化金融论文转化为可执行任务包；(2) 容器化运行时代理端+主机端验证服务，支持可扩展的Agent部署并防止训练-测试泄露；(3) 低延迟数据流设计的论文交易引擎；(4) 长周期和事件市场预测的延迟解析支持；(5) SFT和RL后训练集成。

Result: OpenFinGym在单一执行和验证接口下覆盖了预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等多个任务，解决了现有平台任务碎片化的问题。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym通过统一的多任务环境、自动化构建管道和防泄露的运行时设计，为量化金融Agent开发提供了更全面、可靠的评估平台。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [177] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文正式定义了提示组合型智能体系统中的"组合行为泄漏"(CBL)现象——编辑一个提示模块会静默地改变其他模块的行为，尽管它们没有共享变量或可执行依赖。通过在部署的求职评估智能体(Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144次试验)上的实验，发现只有内容通道产生可检测的配对效应(Cohen's d=0.63, bootstrap 95%CI排除零)，但不会导致推荐翻转——这是一种次阈值干扰，对标准QA不可见，但会在智能体做出的数千次决策中累积。CBL与已知的智能体故障轴正交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示组合型智能体系统在实际部署中反复出现一种故障模式：编辑一个提示模块会静默地改变其他模块的行为，这种干扰在标准QA中不可见，但会在大量决策中累积，需要被正式定义和研究。

Method: 通过可重复的三通道协议（体积、内容、形式）扰动非焦点模块，在部署的求职评估智能体(Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144次试验)上进行实验，并计算Cohen's d和bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 只有内容通道产生可检测的配对效应(Cohen's d=0.63, bootstrap 95%CI排除零)；没有推荐翻转——属于次阈值干扰；CBL与对抗性注入、认知退化、多智能体故障传播、隐私泄漏等已知故障轴正交。

Conclusion: 提供了操作定义、可重复协议、可证伪预测集和系统级表征，建立了跨模块干扰测量作为提示组合型智能体评估的必要条件。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules.
